AIR INDTA 

Air India Staff Homing Regulations, 1967 

HQ/58-5.—In exercise of the powers conferred by Scc- 
.tfon 45(i) of the Air Corporations Act. 1953 (27 of 1953), 
Air Tndia hereby makes the following regulations further to 

amend the Air India Stall Housing Regulations, 1967, as 
follows : 

1. (i) These regulations may be called the Air India stall 
Housing (Amendment ) Regulations, 1986 

(ii) They shall come into force from 1st October. 1986. 

2. In the Air Tndia Staff Housing Regulations, 1967 for 
the existing Regulations Q(l)(a). 9(1) (b), 16(1), 21, 21(a) 
and 25(I) the following Regulations shall be substituted 
namely : 

9. Loan Limits (Individual Loans) 

(I) No individual loan shall normnlly exceed 75 times 
the basic pay plus Variable Dearness Allowance or 
the employee or Rs. 1,25.000/- at the time he/she 
applies for the loan to meet the cdst of dwelling 


(ii) Where the individual loan applied for is more than 
Rs. 1,25.000/- the amount of loan shall not exceed 
60 times the monthly basic pay plus Vatinble Dear¬ 
ness Allowance of the employee at the lime he/shc 
applies for the loan or 90% of the total cost of the 
dwelling housing (inclusive of cost of land ) which¬ 
ever is less subject to a maximum of Rs. 3.00 lakhs, 
in any one ease. 

16. Repayment of loan (Individual) 

(1 ) The loan granted to an employee under these 
regulations, together with interest thereon shall be repaid 
in full by monthly instalments within a period not 
exceeding 20 years or before the employees retirement 
whichevei is earlier. Firstly, recovery of the loan shall 
be made in not more than 180 monthly instalments and 
the interest shall be recovered in not more than 
60 monthly instalments. 

21. loan Limits (Collective Loan) 

(i) A collective housing loan shall be determined on 
. the basis of a maximum of Rs. 3.00 lnkh» per 
house or flat, provided that the total amount of 
loan shall not exceed 90% of the total cost of the 
project of the Co-operative Housing Society inclu¬ 
sive of the cost of land, 

(ii) Where the Co-operative Housing Societies are ex¬ 
clusively formed by employees of the Corporation, 
individually drawing basic pay of less than 
Rs. 1,000'- per month, the collective housing lean 
shall he determined on the basis of a maximum 
of Rs. 1.25,000/- per member employee or collec¬ 
tive housing loan equivalent to 100% of the cost 
of the project, whichever is jcss. 

25. Repayment of loan (Collective) 

(1) The Collective Housing Loan granted to the Society 
under these Regulations, together with interest thereon 
shall be repaid from the date of commencement of re¬ 
payment as hereinafter set forth — 

(a) The recovery of the loan shall be made in not more 
than 180 monthly instalments and the interest shall 
be recovered in not more than 60 monthly instal¬ 
ments. 

S. NARA VAN,SWAMY 
Secretary
